Catalog number : M00246
Uniform title: agnikāryapaddhati
Manuscript : Manuscript no.: 781 Research Library J. and K. Govt. Shrinigar
Description:
A short liturgical manual from Kashmir on fire sacrifice. Contains very interesting incidental information on the deities worshipped in Kashmir. Some are main stream Kaula Tantra of the early period, such as Siddhalakṣmī who was the tutelary goddess of the Malla kings in Nepal.
Notes:
Data entered by the staff of Muktabodha under the supervision of Mark S. G. Dyczkowski.

Revision 0: Feb. 5, 2012
Publication country : India

oṃ śrīmahārājñī bhagavatyai namaḥ || 

oṃ śrīgaṇādhipataye namaḥ |

atha vistare satyagnikāryapaddhatirlikhyate |

karaiścaturbhiḥ patito vahantīṃ sarasvatīṃ siddhikarīṃ namāmi ||

saṃyata kuntalaḥ svastyakādyāsana pūrvaṃ pūrvaśiraskordhvalomādyajinādyāsanopaviṣṭaḥ 
sugandhigandhaliptāṅgaḥ suprasanaḥ subhāvitaḥ sudhūpitaḥ sutāmbūlaścandanāguru carcitaḥ 
hārakaṭakakeyūrāṅkita vigrahaḥ || tatra ||

iti mantreṇa sugandhigandha liptāṅgaḥ mṛtyuñjayena sudhūpitaḥ svatāmbulaśca || 

amṛteśvarabhairavamāṃ rakṣa 3 || 

iti mantreṇa hārakaṭakakeyūrāṅkitaḥ || 

jānubhyāṃ dharaṇiṃ gatvā praṇamya pūjita śambhoriti vistāraḥ kriyate || 

ṣaṭtriṃśadaṅgulāṃ kalaśavediṃ kṛtvā ||

tatra nair-ṛtye astrārghapātraṃ kṛtvā tena sarvadravya prokṣaṇam | 

oṃ phaṭ sarvadravyopacāra śuddhirastu phaṭ svāhā || punaḥ svaiḥ svairmantraiḥ puṣpādi saṃskuryāt || 

āṃ dhūmra 2 jīva 2 saḥ 2 sarvāsāṃ puṣpajātīnāṃ jīva āgaccha tu svāhā || 

iti puṣpamantraḥ ||

jagajjyotīrūpāya dīpāya namaḥ dīpe || 

gandhamādinigandha jīvāpaya tu svāhā candane || 

p. 2) 

hrīṃ svāhā kuṅkume || 

oṃ lāṃ pṛthivyai dharitriśaktaye namaḥ arghe || 

oṃ vlīṃ varuṇa rājānakāya namaḥ || udake ||

p. 2b)

īśe || yāgādbahissvavāme pakṣarūpe || 

oṃ hlāṃ brahmaṇe namaḥ || oṃ ottāna pādātmajāya dhrūvāya namaḥ || yāgādbahiḥ svadakṣe svastikopari || oṃ

p. 3) 

rūṃ agnaye śaktihastāya namaḥ || 

oṃ ṭūṃ yamāya daṇḍahastāya namaḥ || oṃ kṣūṃ nair-ṛte khaḍgāhastāya namaḥ || oṃ vūṃ varuṇāya 
pāśahastāya namaḥ || oṃ yūṃ vāyave dhvajahastāya namaḥ || oṃ kuṃkuvīrāya gadāhastāya namaḥ || oṃ mūṃ 
īśānāya triśūlahastāya namaḥ || oṃ āṃ vrāṃ vraṃ brahmaṇe padmahastāya namaḥ || oṃ hlāṃ viṣṇave 
candrahastāya namaḥ || 

itthaṃ pūjita śambhoḥ jānubhyādharaṇīṃ gatvā praṇamya vijñaptiṃ kṛtvājñāṃ grāhayet || 

pūrvamevatva yā śambho niyuktohaṃ śivādhvare || guruḥ parāpare sthitvā lokānugraha kṛdbhava || 

tvacchaktyāghrāta hṛdayastadayaṃ bhagavanmayā || dṛṣṭodāsyānugrahārthaṃ pravṛttohaṃ na lobhataḥ || 

tvamevacātrabhagavanpramāṇaṃ sarvakarmaṇām || sākṣirūpaḥ svayaṃkartā paramaḥ śaṅkaraḥ śivaḥ || 

tadviśeṣeṇamāmeva samadhiṣṭhāya sannidhim || kuruṣva yena tatpāda samānakriyatāmiyām || 

tvadājñāṃ hṛdaye kṛtvā karomi nikhilāḥ kriyāḥ || anyathā kartumīśā hi vidyeśā api kiṃ prabho || 

vibhogavedayiṣyāmi yadyatkarmakaromyaham || 

ityājñāṃ śambhorgṛhītvā ||

oṃ vṛṣabhāya namaḥ ||

oṃ prabhāmaṇḍalāya namaḥ ||

namaḥ || svadakṣe vāmaśākhāyāṃ || oṃ mahākālāya namaḥ || oṃ yamunāya namaḥ || chāgalāsyāya namaḥ ||

p. 4) 

tataḥ svapaśce dvāramadhye nyāse || 

huṃ phaṭkāra prayogena nārāca mudrayā || 

taddhyānamāha || 

nārācāstraṃ sūryanibhaṃ raudraṃ netratrayojjayojjvalam || śūlapāśadharaṃ vighnaharaṃ tanmuyāsmaret || 

iti yāgadhāsāntaḥ puṣpaṃ prakṣipya || 

pārṣṇāhati trayeṇa bhūmigatāṃstālatrayeṇāntarikṣagatāndivyadṛṣṭyāvalokena divyān || choṭikābhiśca 
digvidiksthān vighnān huṃ phaṭkāra prayogena nivārya || 

tatra vakṣyamāṇa bhūtabaliṃ datvā || 

bhūtāniyānyeva vasanti bhūtale baliṃ gṛhītvā vidhivat prayuktam || 

anyatra vāsaṃ parikalpayantu kṣamantu cānyatra namostutebhyaḥ || 

iti paṭhitvā bhūtāpasarpaṇaṃ kṛvā || 

uttaradiśi sadhyānaṃ śalabha rudraṃ saśaktikaṃ smṛtvā tanmantreṇa taṃ pūjayet || 

oṃ namobhagavate śalabhaśālvāya pakṣirājāya mama śatrūnnāśaya 2 hauṃ gha gha huṃ phaṭ svāhā || 

śalabharudraśrī pādukāṃ pūjayāmi namaḥ || 

tadvāme || 

oṃ namo bhagavati śalabhiśvāli mama śatrūnnāśaya 2 huṃ phaṭ svāhā || 

śalabha devī śrīpādukāṃ pūjayāmi namaḥ | 

oṃ sahasrāra huṃ phaṭ śrīsudarśanāstrāya namaḥ | 

iti pūrvadiśi || 

oṃ hrīṃ 2 prasphura 2 sphura 2 ghoraghoratara tanurūpa naṭa 2 pracaṭa 2 karṣa 2 ghātaya 2 huṃ phaṭ astrāya 
phaṭ aghorāstrāya namaḥ || 

iti dakṣiṇe || 

uttiṣṭha puruṣi kiṃ supiṣibhayaṃ me samupasthitaṃ yadi śakyama śakya tanme bhagavati śamaya svāhā || 

p. 4b) 

vanadurgāyai namaḥ || iti paścime || 

pāśupatāstraṃ vidikṣu || ślīṃ paśu hūṃ phaṭ svāhā || gaṇapatirmadhye smṛtvārcayet || anantaraṃ 
praviśya | tatra lokapālānāṃ vajrādyastra prakāraṃ makharakṣārthaṃ saṃcintya || 

dakṣadiksthaḥ saumyāsyā uttarābhimukhaḥ pāpaploṣasāmarthyādaghoravaktrasāmmukhyenopaviśya || kalaśavedyagre 
vāstu puruṣaṃ pūjayet || 

dhyānamāha || 

sitaṃ tryakṣaṃ saumyamukhaṃ dvibhujaṃ vāstu puruṣam || akṣamālāśūlakaraṃ cintayedvighnanāśanam ||

tataḥ kalaśavedau pūrvādi krameṇa bhūtapūjā || oṃ lāṃkṣitaye namaḥ || oṃ rauṃ agnaye namaḥ || oṃ sauṃ 
yajamānāya namaḥ || oṃ sauṃ sūryāya namaḥ || oṃ vauṃ jalāya namaḥ || oṃ yauṃ vāyave namaḥ || oṃ sauṃ 
somāya namaḥ || oṃ hauṃ ākāśāya namaḥ || tataḥ karanyāsamārabhet || 

gandhadigdhau karau kṛtvā astreṇa prokṣitau kavacenāvaguṇṭhitau cāmṛtenāplāvayet || 

yathā || 

kandasthānādutthitāṃ śaktiṃ dhyātvā dvādaśāntaṃ yathā prāptāṃ cintayitvā tatsthānādutthitāṃ 
śaktiṃ dhyātvā yathā bindusthānaṃ prāptāṃ cintayitvā tatsthānāda mṛtaṃ gṛhītvānāsikya dvādaśāntena 
nirgatāṃ dhyātvā karāvāplāvitau bhāvayet || dhyāyeśca || 

anantaraṃ padmamudrāvaddhvā aṅguṣṭhamūle ādhāraśaktiragre padmaṃ tadupari mūrtiṃ tatra śrīmannetra 

p. 5) 

bhaṭṭārakamaṅguṣṭhādi kaniṣṭhāntamaṅgapañcakaṃ | astraṃ nakheṣu | 

dakṣi tarjanyārabhya vāmatarjanyantaṃ bhairavāṣṭakaṃ | vāmatarjanyā ārabhya dakṣiṇa tarjanyantaṃ 
bhairavāṣṭakam || 

tato niṣkalaṃ sāṅgaṃ devīkaṃ nyasedaṅguṣṭhayoḥ || iti karanyāsaḥ || 

atha prāṇāyāmaḥ || 

oṃ laṃ recakaṃ karomi namaḥ | ityaśuddhaṃ prāṇaprāṇavāyuṃ dvādaśa praṇavoccārakālaṃ 
yāvaddakṣiṇanāsāpuṭena bahiḥ kṣipet || 

oṃ vaṃ pūrakaṃ karomi namaḥ | śuddhaṃ svetaṃ prāṇavāyuṃ caturviṃśatipraṇavoccārakālaṃ 
yāvadantarvāmanāsāpuṭena praviśet || 

oṃ raṃ kumbhakaṃ karomi namaḥ | 

tameva prāṇa vāyuṃ jalabhyata kumbhavadacalasthityā ṣaṭtriṃśatpraṇavoccāraṇa kālaṃ yāvadantarnirodhayet 
|| 

yaṃ ūrdhvarecakaṃ karomi namaḥ | tameva kumbhitaṃ hṛtkaṇṭhatālubhrūmadhya brahmarandhra bhedana krameṇa novā 
śakyatvāśśaniḥ śanairvāmanāsāpuṭena bahiḥ kṣipet || ityupakarṣaḥ || 

oṃ haṃ vyomātmānaṃ sthāpayāmi namaḥ | 

hṛtpadmasthitamātmānaṃ khadyotanibhaṃ sūkṣmaṃ sphurattārakākāraṃ hṛtkaṇṭhatālu 
bhrūmadhyabrahmārandhrabhedanakrameṇordhvarecakena dvādaśānte niyojyamanohaṅkārabuddhiḥ paratatve vilopya 
śūnyaṃ śarīraṃ dhyātvā viśramet || iti sūkṣmaśarīraśuddhiḥ || ityapakarṣaḥ || 

oṃ kṣyūṃ imāṃtanuṃ śoṣayāmi namaḥ || iti sūryarūpaṃ praṇavaṃ śarīra vyāpakaṃ sañcintya || 

iti pārthivīdhāraṇā || 

rakṣaraya-ūṃ tanuṃdāhayāmi namaḥ | iti dakṣiṇapādāṅguṣṭhe kālāgniṃ dhyātvā tajjvālābhiḥ śarīraṃ 
sabāhyābhyantaraṃ bhasmībhūtaṃ bhāvayet || 

ityāgneyīdhāraṇā || 

yūṃ tano utplavanaṃ karomi namaḥ | iti śārīraṃ bhasmavāyunā daśadiggataṃ bhāvayet || vāyavī || 

oṃ vaṃ tanumāplāvayāmi namaḥ | iti dvādaśāntapatitena śāktāmṛtajalena tatsthānaṃ kṣālitaṃ bhāvayet || 

iti vāruṇī || 

oṃ haṃ saṃ vinmayosmināhamasminacānyosti dhyeyaṃ cātra na vidyate || 

ānandapadasaṃlīnaṃ manaḥ samarasī gatam || ityākāśī || 

evaṃ dhāraṇāpañcakaṃ kṣityādyākāśaparyantaṃ bhūtapañcakaṃ puramāṇutayā brahmādi sadāśiva paryantaṃ 
kāraṇapañcakaṃ śrotrādi karaṇa pañcakaṃ sadyojātādi vaktrapañcakaṃ jāgradādyavasthā pañcakaṃ evaṃ 
yatkiñcitprapañcaṃ tatparamaśive śūnyātiśūnye paraprakāśa svarūpe kalpanājālarahite guṇātīte ṣaṣṭhe 
paramākāśaśāyini dvādaśāntasthe śrīmanniṣkalabhaṭṭārake līnaṃ bhāvayet || 

yathā viṣasyaiva mārakatvaṃ mantraśaktyā nivāryate | na tu tatsvarūpaṃ | tathā dehāhaṃ bhāvasya vilopaḥ na 
tu svarūpanāśaḥ | iti bhāvaḥ || 

evaṃ ṣāṭkośike śarīre nivṛtte paramānandamayamātmānaṃ bhāvayet || 

śivosmyahameveti bhāvaḥ || 

p. 6) 

oṃ hāṃ divyāṃ tanuṃ sañjīvayāmi namaḥ | karmāt prabhṛti āsana pakṣaṃ sañcintya || 

ādhāraśaktyai namaḥ || kande || dhātryai namaḥ || nābhyāvadhi || payorṇavāya namaḥ || nābhau || mūlāya 
namaḥ || kandāya namaḥ || nālāya namaḥ || hṛdayāvadhi || 

hṛdidharmādicaraṇasahitaṃ yogapīṭhāsanaṃ saṃcintyatadupari madhye | sitakamalāsanamaṣṭapatraṃsake 
sarakarṇikaṃ dhyāyet || 

pattrebhyo namaḥ || sūryamaṇḍalāya namaḥ || patrāṇi śvetāni dhyāyet || tadadhi pataye brahmaṇe namaḥ || 

brahmāṇaṃ caturmukhaṃ caturbhujaṃ dhyāyet || kesarebhyo namaḥ || somamaṇḍalāya namaḥ || kesarāṇi

dhyāyet || tata īśvarāya namaḥ || īśvaraṃ śuklaṃ dhyāyet || tataḥ sadāśivākhyaṃ pretāsanaṃ |

sadāśivāya namaḥ ||

p. 17)

vīṇāṃ ca daṇḍa sahitāṃ paridhārayantaṃ vāme caturbhujayutaṃ makharakṣaṇārtham ||

vāme sa kheṭaka sa pāśa samanvitaṃ taṃ vṛṣyāsana sthitamahaṃmakharakṣaṇārtham || 

āvāhayāmi varuṇaṃ śaśikhaṇḍamauli pūjāparaṃ ca sṛṇikhaḍgādharaṃ svadakṣe || 

vāme sa kheṭaka sa pāśasamanvitaṃ taṃ nakrāsanasthitamahaṃ makharakṣaṇārtham || 

āvāhayāmi dhṛtavāyupuṭaṃ svahasta yugmena dīkṣitamīśaparaṃ ca vāyum || 

vāme tathākṣa sahitaṃ ca kamaṇḍaluṃ ca cañcanmṛgāsanagataṃ makharakṣaṇārtham || 

āvāhayāmi dhanadaṃ naravāhanasthaṃ cintāmaṇiṃ nijakareṇa tathākṣasūttram || siṃhadhvajena sahitaṃ 
śaśipustakaṃ ca vibhrantamīśvara paraṃ makharakṣaṇārtham || 

āvāhayāmi śaśikhaṇḍa kirīṭamīśaṃmakṣaṃ triśūlamapi dakṣabhuje vahantam || 

vā liṅgadarpaṇa karaṃ ca tathaiva vāme go vāhanaṃ trinayanaṃ makharakṣaṇārtham || 

āvāhayāmi kamalāsanasaṃsthitaṃ taṃ mudrāṃ śrūteḥ parivahantamathākṣasūttram || 

daṇḍaṃ kamaṇḍalumatho satataṃ vahantaṃ brahmāṇamīśvaraparaṃ makharakṣaṇārtham || 

āvāhayāmi satataṃ nijadakṣabhāge vibhrantamakṣasahitaṃ ca phalaṃ ca viṣṇum || 

vā liṅgasaṃyutamatho musulaṃ ca vāme kurmāsanaṃ śivāparaṃ makharakṣaṇārtham || 

āvāhanamatho kuryādgrahāṇāmanu pūrvaśaḥ || āvāhitāgrahāssarve hyādityādyāśśatena va || 

āvāhayāmi dinanāthamahaṃ vahantaṃ dakṣekṣasūttramatha dhūmrapadaṃ dhvajaṃ ca || 

saptāśugaṃ karayugena ca rasmipuñjaṃ tryakṣaṃ trinetraśaraṇaṃ makharakṣaṇārtham || 

āvāhayāmi śaśinaṃ paridhārayantaṃ dakṣekṣasūtramathadhūmrapadaṃ dhvajaṃ ca || 

lagnaṃ mṛge karayugena ca raśmipuñjatryakṣaṃ trinetraśaraṇaṃ makharakṣaṇārtham || 

āvāhayāmi dharaṇī tanayaṃ vahantaṃ dakṣekṣasūttra valayaṃ śikhivāhanastham || 

vāmekamaṇḍaludharaṃ dhṛtaraktavarṇaṃ tryakṣaṃ trinetraśaraṇaṃ makharakṣaṇārtham || 

āvāhayāmi sitamātu kirīṭahāraṃ dakṣekṣasūtravalayaṃ garuḍāsanastham || 

vāme kamaṇḍaludharaṃ sitavarṇaramyaṃ tryakṣaṃ trinetraśaraṇaṃ makharakṣaṇārtham || 

āvāhayāmi dhṛtameva guruṃ surāṇāṃ dakṣekṣasūttravalayaṃ gajavāhanastham || 

vāmekamaṇḍaludharaṃ dhṛtapītavarṇaṃ tryakṣaṃ trinetraśaraṇaṃ makharakṣaṇārtham || 

āvāhayāmi kavimeśśvararūpamugraṃ dakṣekṣasūttravalayaṃ kapivāhanastham || 

p. 18)

somātmaja namastubhyaṃ graharājāyate namaḥ || 

p. 85b) 

bhava paṅkanimagnasya dehi śaṃ rohiṇī suta || 4 || 

vācaspate surācārya namaste tvā mayā paha || pāpasya buddhihīnasya sadā sānugraho bhava || 5 || 

vibudhārigurokāvya namaste karuṇā para || daśāntargrahapīḍādīnyutvā pāhibhyagossuta || 6 || 

namaḥ kṛṣṇaśarīrāya sūryātmaja namostute || saṃsarabhayabhītasya trāṇaṃ kuru śanaiścara || 7 || 

saiṃhikeya namastubhyaṃ grahapīḍāṃ nivāraya || duḥkha pāvaka dagdhasya trāṇaṃ kuru vidhuntuda || 8 || 

sūryādayaḥ ketu yutāḥ śivabhakti parāyaṇāḥ || rakṣantu yajamānasya dharmamāyussuhṛtsutān || 9 || 

atha lokapālakṣamā || 

bhagavandevarājatvaṃ mayāsaṃkhedito makhe || pūjāṃ gṛhāṇa deveśa kṣamyatāṃ kṣamyatāṃ prabho || 1 || 

bhagavanbhagavanvahnetvaṃ mayā khedito makhe || pūjāṃ gṛhāṇa deveśa kṣamyatāṃ kṣamyatāṃ prabhoḥ || 2 || 

bhagabandha parājatvaṃ mayāsaṃkhedito makhe || śivapūjāṃ gṛhāṇatvaṃ kṣamyatāṃ kṣamyatāṃ prabho || 3 || 

bhagavannir-ṛterakṣaḥ ghorātsaṃsārasāgarāt || saṃkheditosimamaye yāhi dhāmasvakaṃ prabho || 4 || 

bhagavanvaruṇādyatvaṃ yajñe śrī śivapūjite || kheditośi mayā deva yāmi svadhāma pāvanam || 5 || 

bhagavanbhavaganvāyo kheditaḥ śāmbhave makhe || tvaṃ mayārcāṃ gṛhītve māṃ yāhi dhāma svake cchayā || 6 || 

bhagabandha na dādyatvaṃ kheditaḥ śāṅkare kratau || gṛhītvārcāṃ mayā dattāṃ kṣamyatāṃ kṣamyatāṃ 
prabho || 7 || 

bhagavandevadevetvaṃ mīśānārcāṃ svake makhe || 

p. 86) 

gṛhītvā dehi me sarva puruṣārthaṃ prayāhi ca || 8 || 

bhagavaṃstrijagatpūjya sarvalokapitāmaha || saive dhvare kṛtā rakṣā tvayātat kṣamyatāṃ prabho || 9 || 

bhagavanbhagavanviṣṇo kṛta dānava nāśana || śaive dhvare kṛtārakṣā tvayā tatkṣamyatāṃ prabho || 10 || 

bhagavanto grahāssarve yenye copagrahā matāḥ || gṛhṇantu pūjāṃ maddhattāṃ tuṣṭā yāntu svakaṃ padam || 11 
|| 

kapāleśādya bhagavankheditaḥ cakra pūjitaḥ || śaive dhvare mayātyarthaṃ bhairavendra namostute || 1 || 

śāṅkaredyā mayā yajñe bhagavāñśikhivāhana || pūjitaḥ khedito sitvaṃ tuṣṭo yā hi svakaṃ padam || 2 || 

bhagavankrodharājatvaṃ dharmā yajana pūjita || kṛtārakṣāmakhe śaive yā hi svaṃ vimalaṃ padam || 3 || 

bhagavanvikarālatvaṃ sadānir-ṛti pūjita || śaivīṃ pūjāṃ gṛhāṇatvaṃ yā hi svaṃ vimalaṃ padam || 4 || 

bhagavanmanmatheśatvaṃ varuṇena kṛtārcana || kheditaḥ śivayajñedyakṣasyatāṃ kṣamyatāṃ prabho || 5 || 

bhagavanmeghanādatvaṃ vāyunā pūjitānagha || śaive yajñe kheditastvaṃ kṣamyatāṃ kṣamyatāṃ prabho || 6 ||

śambho śivajagannātha bhagavankhedito mayā || 

p. 86) 

tvaṃ devo devadevānāṃ tvamevaparamāgatiḥ || 

tvaṃ bhuktimuktike dātā kāraṇānāṃ ca kāraṇam || tvayi tuṣṭhe surā tuṣṭāḥ sakalāḥ santu nityaśaḥ || 

airāvaṇa samārūḍho hemavarṇo mahādyutiḥ || devendraḥ satataṃ tuṃṣṭaḥ parame śārcane rataḥ || 

śiva dhyānaika nirataḥ śivasadbhāvabhāvitaḥ || śivapraṇāmaparamaḥ karo tu mama śāntikam || 1 || 

bhūtikṛddehināṃ devo jvalanaḥ pāpanāśanaḥ || śivapūjājapodyuktaḥ śivasadbhāvabhāvitaḥ || 

śāntiṃ karotu me devastathā pāpaparikṣayam || 2 || 

antakotha mahātejāśśivadhyānaparāyaṇaḥ || śivapūjā samāyuktaḥ kṣemārogyaṃ karo tu me || 3 ||

varuṇaḥ parayābhaktyā śivaikagatamānasaḥ || rogaśokāgni santāpaṃ nirnāśaya tu me sadā || 5 || 

pavanaḥ paramo devaḥ parameśvara bhāvitaḥ || kṣemārogyabaliṃ śāntiṃ karo tu satataṃ mama || 6 || 

kuvero varadaḥ śrīmānharapādārcane rataḥ || śāntiṃ karo tu me prītaḥ śāntaḥ śānte na tejasā || 7 || 

īśānaḥ paramo devaḥ sarvadevottamottamaḥ || sopi sarvātma bhāvena śāntimāśukarotu me || 8 || 

bhuloketha bhuvoloke svarloke vivasanti ye || devādivya prabhā yuktāḥ śāntiṃ kurvantu me sadā || 9 ||

p. 87) 

satyaloke ca ye devāḥ svaprabhojjvalavigrahāḥ || śivabhaktāḥ sumanaso bhayaṃ nirnāśayantu me || 11 || 

karacaraṇa kṛtaṃ vākāyajaṃ karṇajaṃ vā śravaṇa nayanajaṃvāmā na saṃvāparādham || 

vidhitamavidhitaṃ vā sarvametatkṣmasva jayajaya karuṇābdhe śrīmahādevaśambho || 

kṣamasvamantranāthāya nityānandamayāya ca || dharmārtha kāmamokṣāya punarāgamanāya ca ||

tuṣṭaḥ punarāgamāya || 

ya ekaḥ sarvaviddevaḥ sākṣī viśvasya karmaṇaḥ || brahmādyairapyagamyoyaḥ samayā pūjito makhe ||

ya eva sṛṣṭi saṃrakṣāsaṃhārakaraṇakṣamaḥ || trirūpastriguṇaḥ śambhuḥ samayā pūjito śvakhe || 

anena devadevena nirguṇenātsūrūpiṇā || sā śaktistrividhopāntā samayā pūjito dhvare || 

adya me saphalaṃ janma adya me saphalāḥ kriyāḥ || evaṃ prabhāvo yacchambhuḥ kheditosmiñśivādhvare || 

anātha madhyamāṃ devarakṣāsmātpāpa saṅkaṭām || siddhimṛddhiṃ tathā buddhiṃ śuddhiṃ mānasi kīṃ mayi || 

dehi bhogaṃ ca mokṣaṃ ca manobhilaṣitaṃ mama || sarvatrātha maheśāna dehi siddhiṃ ca śaśvatīm || 

anāmayaṃ padaṃ śambho dehi me parameśvara || idānīṃ paramīśāna kṣamyatāṃ kṣamyatāṃ prabho || 

āyāsitosi yajñesmiṃstuṣṭo yā hi svakaṃ padam || gaṇeśādyāḥ smṛtā yetra dvārapālanakarmaṇi || 

indrādyālokapālāśca hyādityādyāstathā grahāḥ || kapāle śādayo yetra bhairavā bhairavyāśritāḥ || 

saptāvaraṇamantrāśca tathā vastrādi devatāḥ || tathā ṣaḍadhvasandoho gāyatryādyāśca devatāḥ || 

vāstoṣpati prabhṛtayo grahadevādi devatāḥ || vaṭukā herukādyāśca kṣettrapālāśca śāśvatāḥ || 

te sarve vividhānkamāndatvāyāntu svakaṃ padam || āhvānaṃ naiva jānāmi naiva jānāmi pūjanam || 

visarjanaṃ na jānāmi kṣamyatāṃ parameśvara || 

ubhābhyāṃ jānubhyāṃ pāṇibhyāṃ śirasā corasā namaskāraṃ karomi namaḥ || namo brahmaṇetyādi ||

dhyātavyāḥ || tatkalaśagataṃ tejaḥ puñjaṃ saṃhāriṇya sakalabhairava bhaṭṭāraka rūpaṃ niṣkalanāthe

svātmanyatpayāmi namaḥ || 

iti bhogadvādaśāntena gṛhītvā pūrakena hṛtsthaṃ kṛtvā yathā vandana sā tatra pūjayediti guravaḥ || 

atha melāpa vidyāṃ triḥ paṭhet ||

p. 89b) 

oṃ kalaśamaṇḍalasthaṃ bhagavantaṃ kṣamāpayet | ya ekaḥ sarvaviddevaḥ sākṣī viśvasya karmaṇaḥ || 

brahmādyairapya gamyoyasmamayā pūjito dhvare | sakalo niṣkalo devo dvādaśānta padaṃ sthitaḥ || 

sa devo bhagavāñśambhuḥ kheditosmin kriyā vidhau | ya eva sṛṣṭi saṃrakṣā saṃhārakaraṇa kṣamaḥ || 

trirūpastriguṇaśśambhussamayā pūjito dhvare | ye neva devadevena nirguṇenātmarūpiṇā || 

sāśaktistrīvidho pāttā samayā pūjito dhvare | asya me saphalaṃ janma adya me saphalāḥ kriyāḥ || 

evaṃ prabhāvo yacchambhuḥ pūjitosminmayā dhvare | tvamevabhagavānsākṣādekaḥ saṃsāratārakaḥ || 

bhuktimukti pradaścaika eka evākhilāśrayaḥ | anāthamadyamāṃ devarakṣāsmātpāpasaṅkaṭāt || 

siddhimṛddhiṃ tathā buddhiṃ śuddhiṃ mānasikīṃ mayi | dehi bhogaṃ ca mokṣaṃ ca manobhilaṣitaṃ dhrūvam || 

sarvathātra maheśāna dehi siddhiṃ ca śāśvatīm | anāmaya padaṃ śambho dehi me parameśvara || 

idānīṃ parameśāna kṣamyatāṃ kṣamyatāṃ prabho | āyāsitosi yajñesmiṃstuṣṭo yā hi svakaṃ padam ||

yatpūrvajanmani mayāvarta māne ca janmani | pāpaṃ kṛtaṃ karomyatra tasmāttāḥ yame prabho || 

saṃsārasāgarāntasthaṃ komāmuddhartu marhati | nṛte madvacanasyāsya karṇadhārācchivātprabho || 

aghoraghorasaṃsāra *?rāpaharaṇodyataḥ | paramānandadātāsau bhūyājjanmani janmani || 

athavā janmaśaṅkāpi na me śiva prasādataḥ | dīkṣānirdagdha pāśasya bandhanaṃ me kutaḥ prabho || 

sc 92)

sc 92) 

oṃ śrī gurubhyo namaḥ | oṃ namāmi satguruṃ śāntaṃ pratyakṣaṃ śivarūpiṇam | śirasāyogapīṭhasthaṃ

muktidvārasya sopānaṃ tasmai śrī |

navāya navarūpāya paramārthaika rūpiṇe | sarvāñāna tamo bheda bhāvavecidghanāyate || 

svatantrāya dayātkḷptavigrahāya parātmane | paratantrāya bhaktānāṃ bhavyānāṃ bhavyarūpiṇe || 

jñānināṃ jñānarūpāya prakāśāya prakāśinām | vivekināṃ vivekāya vimarśāya vimarśinām || 

purasthātpārśvayoḥ pṛṣṭhe namaskuryāmuparyadhaḥ | sadā saścittarūpeṇa vidhe hi bhavadāsanam || 

oṃ śrī devyuvāca || 

prāyaścitta vihīnasya phalaṃ bhogāya kevalam | sarvasākṣī mahādevo narakānarakā na sṛjatpurā | 

sc 93) 

tasmātsarvaprayatnena prāyaścittamihaiva tu kṛtasya karmaṇaḥ kuryādanulopo bhavedyadi | 

pramādādi mahādoṣa pravilāpana kāraṇam | prāyaścittaṃ paraṃ satyaṃ śrī guroḥ pādukā smṛtiḥ || 

tvayaivoktaṃ maheśāna kathaṃ smartavyamīśvara | vadame kṛpayā deva śrīguroḥ pādukā smṛtiḥ || 

oṃ śrī bhairava uvāca || 

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi śrīguroḥ pādukāstutim | prāyaścitta vihīnānāṃ sarvapāpaharaṃ kalau || 

oṃ yasya śrīpādaraja sārañjate mastake śivaḥ | ramate saha pārvatyā tasya śrīpādukā stutiḥ || 

homohatamasākrāntamuddharatyandha kūpataḥ | 

kevalaṃ kṛpayā nātha tasya śrī0 | 

yasya trailokyamakhilaṃ saścidānanda lakṣaṇam || pūryate svaguṇaiḥ śaktyā tasya śrī0 

yasya trailokyamakhilaṃ vaśītiṣṭhatya śeṣata | 

svadṛṣṭipātamātreṇa tasya śrī0 | 

yasmai sarvasva mātmānamekī kṛtyāpi bhaktitaḥ | 

samarpayati tacchiṣyaḥ tasya śrī0 | 

yamāśratyātma vijñānaṃ savide mṛgayā mahe | 

haitukairmūṣitaṃ doṣaistasya śrī0 | 

yasmin sṛṣṭi sthiti dhvaṃsa pidhānānugrahātmakam | 

prakāśate pañcakṛtyaṃ tasya śrī0 | 

hṛdi mūle lalāṭe ca dvādaśānte parā śivaḥ | 

yasyājñāṃ pālayannāste tasya śrī0 | 

ādyante dṛḍhabhedasya paśoḥ pāśavimocanam | 

yamāhuḥ śāmbhavī vācastasya śrī0 || 

yasya pādatale sindhuḥ pādāgre kulapa hi dhāma svake cchayā 6 | 

sc 93) 

bhagabandhanadādyatvaṃ kheditaḥ śāṅkare kṛtau | gṛhītvārcāṃ mayādattāṃ kṣamyatāṃ 2 prabho 7 || 

bhagavandevadevatvamīśānārcāṃ svake makhe | gṛhītvā dehi me sarva puruṣārthaṃ prathā hi ca 8 || 

bhagavaṃstri jagatpūjya sarvaloka pitāmaha | śaive dhvare kṛtārakṣā tvayā tatkṣamyatāṃ prabho 9 || 

bhagavanbhagavanviṣṇo kṛtadānavanāśana | śaive dhvare kṛtārakṣā tvayā tat kṣamyatāṃ prabho 10 || 

bhagavanto grahāḥ sarve yenye copagrahā matāḥ | grahṇīta pūjāṃ maddattāṃ tuṣṭāyāta svakaṃ padam || 

kapāleśādya bhagavankheditaḥ śakra pūjita | śaive dhvare mayā tyarthaṃ bhairavendra namostute || 1 || 

śāṅkaredya mayā yajñe bhagavañśikhi vāhana pūjitaḥ | kheditosi tvāṃ tuṣṭo yā hi svakaṃ padam || 2 || 

bhagavankrodharājatvaṃ dharmarājaprapūjita | kṛtārakṣā makhe śaive yā hi svaṃ vimalaṃ padam || 3 || 

bhagavanvikarālatvaṃ sadānir-ṛta pūjita | śaivīṃ pūjāṃ gṛhāṇatvaṃ yā hi svaṃ vimalaṃ padam || 4 || 

bhagavanmanmathe satvaṃ varuṇena kṛtārcana | kheditaḥ śivayajñesya kṣamyatāṃ 2 prabho || 5 || 

bhagavanmeghanādatvaṃ vāyunā pūjitānagha | śaive yajñe kheditastvaṃ kṣamyatāṃ 2 prabho || 6 || 

bhagavansomarājādya kheditodanadārcita | śaive dhvare kṛtārakṣākṣamyatāṃ 2 prabho || 7 || 

vidyārājādya bhagavañśaivesmin makha karmaṇi | āyāsitosi devatvaṃ kṣamyatāṃ kṣamyatāṃ prabho || 8 || 

saptāvaraṇake muṣminnanantādyāḥ sthitāścaye | te sarve pūjitāstuṣṭā yāntu śaivaṃ paraṃ padam || 

ityāvaraṇakṣamā puṣpāṇi | 

śambho śiva jagannātha bhagavan khedito mayā | tvaṃ devo devadevānāṃ tvameva paramāgatiḥ || 

tvaṃ bhuktimuktikodātā kāraṇānāṃ ca kāraṇam | tvayi tuṣṭe surā tuṣṭāḥ sakalāḥ santi nityaśaḥ || 

bhavatprasādato deva yajñoyaṃ śiva daivataḥ | ṣadudhvadīkṣā saṃjñāśca sampannaḥ śiṣya hetukaḥ || 

idānīṃ noparoddhavyaṃ gaccha deva svaviṣṭapam | 

agnerapi kṣamā puṣpāṇi | 

bhagavanbhagavanvahnetvaṃ surāṇāṃ mukhaṃ param | pratyakṣa devo bhūloke sarvakarma pratārakaḥ || 

antarbahiśca lokānāṃ tvameva kṛta sannidhiḥ | tvayi tuṣṭe surāstuṣṭā ityāhussanakādayaḥ | kheditosi mayā 
yajñe dīkṣākarmaṇi sāmpratam || 

dharmaṃ dānaṃ sutāṃścaiva datvāyā hi svakaṃ padam | 

iti vahneḥ kṣamāpanam || 

atha kalaśadvayotthāpana mantraḥ | 

uttiṣṭha devadevaśa tvaṃ maṇḍala suraiḥ saha | rakṣāsmānpūrvato nityaṃ dhṛta tatpuruṣābhidhaḥ || 

tathā dakṣiṇato rakṣa dhṛtāghorātma bhairavaḥ | tathaivottarato rakṣa vāmadevosi śaṅkara || 

varuṇāśāgato nityaṃ sadyo jātāsirakṣamām | ūrdhvaṃ rakṣamaheśāna tvamīśānosi devatā || 

sarvataḥ sarvathā deva sarvatrāsmin bhavādhvani | pāhimāṃ yā hi deveśa nityānandamayaṃ param || 

iti mantrakaśāstra kalajalaiḥ svātmānaṃ śiṣyaṃścābhiṣiñcet | 

tataḥ śrī paramaśivaśakti pātasu kṛto palabdha śrīmadaghoramantra sā samāṃsaḥ śāka ucyate | 

sc 94b) 

sādhakorcana sā dadyāt suprasannena cetasā | baliṃ saṅkalpya manasā yo varṣāntaṃ na dāsyati || 

tasya vighnakarā devāḥ paśunputrānharantite | tasmātsaṅkalpitaṃ dadyād devebhyo balimuttamām || 

śīghrameva na dadyādvai vighnairantarito yadi | balidānādavāpnoti siddhimiṣṭāṃ ca satvaram || 

tasmāt sarva prayatnena na vilambaṃ hi kārayet | saṅkalpita vilambāddhi prāpyate vighna sañcayam || 

jayaṃ siddhiṃ ca ṛddhiṃ ca śīghraṃ pūrvamukho baliḥ | dhūnanaṃ cenmahādevi tadā sarvaṃ vinirdiśet || 

aiśānyāṃ mantrasiddhisyādāgneyāṃ vijayastathā | dhūnanāśca baleryāsyāṃ mṛtyumantakamādiśet || 

nair-ṛtyāṃ tu samaṃ jñeyaṃ vāruṇyāṃ vighnanāśanam | vāyavyāṃ dhanavṛddhiḥ syāduttarasyāṃ dhrūvo 
jayaḥ || 

anyatrāpi tuṣṭāstu devatāḥ pūrve hyāgne ye bahumaṅgalam | dakṣiṇe mṛtyumāpnoti vāyāvuścāṭanaṃ bhavet 
|| 

kauvīryāṃ tu śriyo nityamaiśānyāṃ jñānameva ca | nair-ṛtye jñātimaraṇaṃ vāruṇe tu mahāphalam || 

evaṃ lakṣaṇamālakṣya paśucchedaṃ samārabhet | yāvanna kampate dehaṃ tāvannaivopa ghātayet || 

paśuśca svargatiṃ yāti ghātīpāpaṃ na vindati | raktaṃ ca kṣetrapālebhyaḥ savyahastayutaṃ śiraḥ || 

iṣṭāya kalpayeddevi yaddhṛtkhaṇḍaṃ tu cakrake | hṛtpadmaṃ yoginībhyaśca datvā prītimavāpnuyāt || 

baliṃ datvā tu devīśi yo vidhiṃ na samācaret | paśuhatyāmavāpnoti ceti satyena teśape || 

adatvā vighnasaṅghāśca prāpnuyāśca pade pade | paśuhatyāmapīśāni siddhindhāniṃ ca sundari || 

svārthe paśubadho hiṃsā yajñe paśuṃ vadho baliḥ | itthaṃ paśubadhaḥ prokto veda tantrānu śāsanam || 

sc 95) 

yajñārthaṃ paśavaḥ sṛṣṭā yajñārthaṃ paśughātanam | atastvāṃ ghātayiṣyāmi tasmādyajña vidho vadhaḥ 
|| 

hastā deva samucchidya yasya tadyoginī haret | tasya sarvatra siddhisyājjayaścaiva na saṃśayaḥ || 

avaśiṣṭabaliṃ kuryuryoginyo vāsā api | sādhakasya maheśāni vighnasaṅghāḥ pade pade || 

vāyasā api deveśi śīghraṃ yadbalimāharet | tasyāpi sādhakasyāśujaya eva na saṃśayaḥ || 

asantarpya tu kṣettreśānyoginyabhyoya ādadet | baliṃ mahānimāpnoti sādhakaḥ śubhalakṣaṇe || 

tasmātsarva prayatnena bhairavamarcayet sadā | 

atha paśupahāralakṣ'nam | 

upahāraṃ pravakṣyāmi vākyena surasundari | yajñe vivāha kāleṣu devyarcāyāṃ surārcane || 

ajāvikamahiṣaśca vapuḥ saṃpūrito bhavet | karṇaṃ suparṇa śṛṅgīnāṃ hīnāṅgādi tuṣṭau?dim || 

gāyatryā cābhimantreṇa paṭhet karṇe tu dakṣiṇe | devatāgre paśuṃ prāpyāsnāpayet parito jalaiḥ || 

anena tu vidhānena mucyate janmanaḥ paśuḥ | śubhaṃ vātra? śubhaṃ vāpi lakṣaṇaṃ tu parīkṣayet || 

śiraścāle svāmi bhayaṃ prārambhe tu yadā bhavet | niṣaddhe tu bhayaṃ vindyād deva doṣo bhaviṣyati || 

mūrdhni vyādhisu? bāhulyaṃ kampite ca hyupaddhrūvam | mūtreṇa tvagni dāhaḥ syādviṣṭipāte dhanakṣayaḥ || 

paścādrāja kulāddhānijayastamagrato bhavet | vāhane nityaduḥkhāni bhrāmaṇe caḥ kulakṣaya | evaṃ cihnaṃ 
parīkṣyaṃ sulakṣaṇe lakṣaṇaṃ mahat || 

na hīnaṃte na jātvaivaṃ devatāgre niyojayet | dātako ghātako bhogī tribhirvadyaṃ prakalpayet || 

anaivedyaṃ na bhoktavyaṃ manarpitaṃ na ghātayet | deyaṃ sarvatra māṃsādi asurāsura kṣetrapān || 

śirastu sarvadevānāmādī bhairava tarpaṇe | tvakṣeva gaṇanāthasya mahākālagaṇḍaram || 

antraivaikṣetrapālānāṃ kāla māṃsaṃ tukṣe carīm viṣṭirya
